[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric).

## Specification
Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU),
module name `tpu`, implementing the EXACT per-cycle behavior of the logikbench
`tpu` benchmark (zeroasiccorp/logikbench, MIT).

FUNCTION: computes C = A * B tile products on an 8x8 grid of processing
elements. B (the "weight" matrix, 8-bit signed) is loaded into the array and
held; A (the "activation" matrix, 8-bit signed) streams through; result rows
C (32-bit signed) drain out the bottom.

PROTOCOL:
  1. Weight load: assert w_valid for 8 cycles; w_data carries one weight row
     per cycle, 8 signed bytes packed { col 7 ... col 0 }, BOTTOM matrix row
     first (cycle k carries B[7-k][*]; the weights shift south). After 8
     cycles PE(i,j) holds B[i][j].
  2. Compute: assert a_valid while streaming A one row per cycle (packed the
     same way). Row m of the result, C[m][*] packed { col 7 ... col 0 } as 8
     signed 32-bit words, appears on c_data with c_valid high exactly 15
     (= 2*8-1) compute cycles after its A row was accepted.
  - The array advances only while w_valid is low: asserting w_valid stalls
    any in-flight computation (and reloads weights). Zeros are injected while
    a_valid is low, so idle/drain cycles add nothing to the accumulators.
  - Multiple A tiles may stream back-to-back against the same loaded weights.

CYCLE ACCURACY: c_valid is compared against the original design EVERY cycle;
c_data is compared on every cycle where c_valid is high. The 15-cycle
compute-latency and the stall semantics are part of the contract.

Ports:
  clk, rst (async active-high reset, asserted only before the stream begins)
  w_valid, w_data[63:0]
  a_valid, a_data[63:0]
  c_valid, c_data[255:0]

A working starting point is provided: `context/starting_point.v` is the
original logikbench RTL (tpu_pe / tpu_array / tpu: PE mesh, per-row input
skew, per-column output de-skew, valid pipeline). It passes all 830 golden
cycles (328 result rows: directed zero/identity/extreme tiles, random tiles,
back-to-back streams, a_valid gaps, mid-drain weight reloads, random soak).
Study it, then optimize while preserving the cycle-accurate behavior. The
sibling benchmark `tpu_spire` shares the identical testbench and vectors
with a SpireHDL starting point.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.
- done: Signal completion (only use when truly stuck with no more ideas)

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. Only call done if you are truly stuck with no more ideas.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (tool calls / LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// Processing element (PE) for the weight-stationary systolic array (TPU MXU).
//
// Each PE holds one stationary signed weight 'w'. Two independent datapaths
// share the cell:
//
//   Weight load (ld): the weights form a per-column shift register that loads
//   from the top. On each ld cycle the PE captures the weight from the PE above
//   (w_north) and presents its stored weight downward (w_south). After N load
//   cycles every PE in a column holds its final stationary weight.
//
//   Compute (en): the activation flows west to east (registered, one hop per
//   cycle) and the partial sum flows north to south, accumulating the local
//   product a_west * w. This is the only arithmetic cell in the array: one
//   signed DW x DW multiply plus an ACCW-bit add.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu_pe
  #(parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)
   (
    input		  clk,
    input		  rst,	      // synchronous, active high
    input		  ld,	      // weight-load shift enable
    input		  en,	      // compute enable
    input [DW-1:0]	  w_north,    // weight in from PE above
    input [DW-1:0]	  a_west,     // activation in from west
    input [ACCW-1:0]	  psum_north, // partial sum in from above
    output [DW-1:0]	  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below
    output reg [DW-1:0]	  a_east,     // activation out to east
    output reg [ACCW-1:0] psum_south  // partial sum out to below
    );

   // stationary weight register; presented downward for the load shift chain
   reg [DW-1:0] w;
   assign w_south = w;

   // local product: the ONLY signed operation. Operands are interpreted as
   // signed here via $signed; the 2*DW-bit result holds the two's-complement
   // product, which is then sign-extended to the accumulator width below.
   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);

   always @(posedge clk) begin
      if (rst) begin
         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};
         a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};
         psum_south <= {ACCW{1'b0}};
      end
      else begin
         if (ld)
           w <= w_north;
         if (en) begin
            a_east     <= a_west;
            psum_south <= psum_north +
                          {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};
         end
      end
   end

endmodule
//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core).
//
// One tpu_pe per grid element, wired as a 2D mesh (per CLAUDE.md: replicated
// hardware via generate, never a procedural unroll):
//
//   - weights load from the top: w_top[j] feeds column j; each column is a
//     vertical shift register (w_south of PE(i,j) -> w_north of PE(i+1,j)).
//   - activations flow west to east: a_left[i] enters row i; a_east of PE(i,j)
//     feeds a_west of PE(i,j+1).
//   - partial sums flow north to south and accumulate; the top row's psum_north
//     is tied to zero (fresh accumulation), and c_bot[j] is the column-j result
//     leaving the bottom row.
//
// With weight B[i][j] held at PE(i,j) and activation A[m][i] entering row i,
// column j computes C[m][j] = sum_i A[m][i] * B[i][j]. Input/output skew (which
// time-aligns rows and columns) lives in the tpu top, not here.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu_array
  #(parameter N = 8,	 // array dimension (N x N PEs)
    parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator width (signed)
   (
    input		clk,
    input		rst,	// synchronous, active high
    input		ld,	// weight-load shift enable
    input		en,	// compute enable
    input [N*DW-1:0]	w_top,	// weight inputs, one per column (load)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	a_left,	// activation inputs, one per row
    output [N*ACCW-1:0]	c_bot	// column results, one per column
    );

   // mesh nets: a_h carries activations east, psum_v carries sums south,
   // w_v carries weights south during load.
   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];
   wire [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];
   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];

   genvar	   i, j;
   generate
      // west edge: activation inputs into column 0 of each row
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west
         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];
      end
      // top edge: zero partial sums, weight inputs; bottom edge: results out

      // top edge: zero partial sums, weight inputs; bottom edge: results out
      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge
         assign psum_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};
         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];
         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];
      end
      // the PE mesh
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row
         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col
            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe
              (.clk        (clk),
               .rst        (rst),
               .ld         (ld),
               .en         (en),
               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),
               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),
               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),
               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),
               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),
               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));
         end
      end
   endgenerate

endmodule
//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU).
//
// Computes one tile product C = A * B, where A and B are N x N signed operands
// (DW bits) and C is N x N signed (ACCW bits). B is the "weight" matrix, loaded
// into and held by the array; A is the "activation" matrix, streamed through; C
// drains out the bottom. Built from a tpu_array of N x N tpu_pe cells.
//
// Operation has two phases the host drives via valids:
//
//   1. Weight load: assert w_valid for N cycles. w_data carries one weight row
//      per cycle, N signed weights packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }. Because the
//      array loads top-down (shift south), push the BOTTOM matrix row first:
//      cycle k carries B[N-1-k][*]. After N cycles PE(i,j) holds B[i][j].
//
//   2. Compute: assert a_valid while streaming A one row per cycle, a_data
//      packed { col N-1 ... col 0 } = A[m][N-1..0]. Row m of the result, C[m][*]
//      (packed the same way), appears on c_data with c_valid high, 2*N-1 cycles
//      after its A row was accepted.
//
// Time alignment: row i of A is delayed i cycles entering the array (input
// skew) so partial sums chain correctly down each column; column j of the
// result is delayed N-1-j cycles (output de-skew) so a full result row leaves
// together. Both skew networks and the valid pipeline advance only with the
// array (compute enable), so the 2*N-1 latency is exact. Per CLAUDE.md the skew
// chains are generated per-row/column shift registers, not a procedural unroll.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu
  #(parameter N = 8,	 // array dimension (N x N)
    parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // result / accumulator width (signed)
   (
    input		clk,
    input		rst,	 // synchronous, active high
    input		w_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	w_data,	 // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    input		a_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	a_data,	 // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    output		c_valid, // result-row valid
    output [N*ACCW-1:0]	c_data	 // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    );

   // total injection-to-output latency, in compute cycles
   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;

   // load weights while w_valid; otherwise the array computes (and drains)
   wire	      ld = w_valid;
   wire	      en = ~w_valid;

   //##########################################################################
   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i cycles. Zero is injected
   // when a_valid is low so idle/drain cycles add nothing to the accumulators.
   //##########################################################################
   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;
   genvar	   i;
   generate
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew
         wire [DW-1:0] din =
		       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};
         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0
            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;
         end
         else begin : g_skewn
            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];
            integer k;
            always @(posedge clk) begin
               if (rst) begin
                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)
                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};
               end
               else if (en) begin
                  sr[0] <= din;
                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)
                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];
               end
            end
            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];
         end
      end
   endgenerate

   //##########################################################################
   // The systolic array.
   //##########################################################################
   wire [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;
   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array
     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),
      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));

   //##########################################################################
   // Output de-skew: column j is delayed N-1-j cycles so all columns of a
   // result row line up. Column N-1 (longest path) needs no delay.
   //##########################################################################
   genvar j;
   generate
      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew
         localparam D = N - 1 - j;
         wire [ACCW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];
         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0
            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = cin;
         end
         else begin : g_deskewn
            reg [ACCW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];
            integer k;
            always @(posedge clk) begin
               if (rst) begin
                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)
                    dr[k] <= {ACCW{1'b0}};
               end
               else if (en) begin
                  dr[0] <= cin;
                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)
                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];
               end
            end
            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = dr[D-1];
         end
      end
   endgenerate

   //##########################################################################
   // Valid pipeline: c_valid is a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.
   //##########################################################################
   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;
   always @(posedge clk) begin
      if (rst)
        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};
      else if (en)
        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};
   end
   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];

endmodule

MXU)\n// Optimized for area-delay product while maintaining cycle-accurate behavior\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe #(\n    parameter int DW = 8,\n    parameter int ACCW = 32\n)(\n    input  logic                  clk,\n    input  logic                  rst,\n    input  logic                  ld,\n    input  logic                  en,\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]         w_north,\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]         a_west,\n    input  logic [ACCW-1:0]       psum_north,\n    output logic [DW-1:0]         w_south,\n    output logic [DW-1:0]         a_east,\n    output logic [ACCW-1:0]       psum_south\n);\n\n    logic [DW-1:0] w;\n    assign w_south = w;\n\n    logic signed [2*DW-1:0] prod;\n    assign prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n        if (rst) begin\n            w          <= '0;\n            a_east     <= '0;\n            psum_south <= '0;\n        end else Let me analyze the critical path and try optimizing the adder in the PE. The critical path goes through the multiplier and 32-bit adder. Let me implement a faster parallel prefix adder.


  -> create_file(filename='design_opt1.sv', content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (Google TPU-style MXU)\n// Optimized with parallel prefix adder for accumulation\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe #(\n    parameter int DW = 8,\n    parameter int ACCW = 32\n)(\n    input  logic                  clk,\n    input  logic                  rst,\n    input  logic                  ld,\n    input  logic                  en,\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]         w_north,\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]         a_west,\n    input  logic [ACCW-1:0]       psum_north,\n    output logic [DW-1:0]         w_south,\n    output logic [DW-1:0]         a_east,\n    output logic [ACCW-1:0]       psum_south\n);\n\n    logic [DW-1:0] w;\n    assign w_south = w;\n\n    logic signed [2*DW-1:0] prod;\n    assign prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n    // Sign-extended product\n    logic [ACCW-1:0] prod_sext;\n    assign prod_sext = {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n    // Parallel prefix adder (Brent-Kung style) for ACCW-bit addition\n    logic [ACCW-1:0] sum;\n    logic [ACCW-1:0] g, p;\n    logic [ACCW-1:0] g_out, p_out;\n    \n    // Generate/Propagate\n    assign g = prod_sext & psum_north;\n    assign p = prod_sext | psum_north;\n    \n    // Brent-Kung prefix computation\n    localparam int LG = $clog2(ACCW);\n    logic [ACCW-1:0] g_stage [0:LG];\n    logic [ACCW-1:0] p_stage [0:LG];\n    \n    assign g_stage[0] = g;\n    assign p_stage[0] = p;\n    \n    genvar l, k;\n    generate\n        for (l = 1; l <= LG; l++) begin : prefix_levels\n            for (k = 0; k < ACCW; k++) begin : prefix_bits\n                if (k < (1 << (l-1))) begin : white\n                    assign g_stage[l][k] = g_stage[l-1][k];\n                    assign p_stage[l][k] = p_stage[l-1][k];\n                end else begin : black\n                    assign g_stage[l][k] = g_stage[l-1][k] | (p_stage[l-1][k] & g_stage[l-1][k - (1 << (l-1))]);\n                    assign p_stage[l][k] = p_stage[l-1][k] & p_stage[l-1][k - (1 << (l-1))];\n                end\n            end\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    \n    assign g_out = g_stage[LG];\n    assign p_out = p_stage[LG];\n    \n    // Sum = p ^ (carry_in << 1), carry_in = g_out shifted right by 1\n    assign sum = p_out ^ {g_out[ACCW-2:0], 1'b0};\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n        if (rst) begin\n            w          <= '0;\n            a_east     <= '0;\n            psum_south <= '0;\n        end else begin\n            if (ld)\n                w <= w_north;\n            if (en) begin\n                a_east     <= a_west;\n                psum_south <= sum;\n            end\n        end\n    end\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu_array #(\n    parameter int N = 8,\n    parameter int DW = 8,\n    parameter int ACCW = 32\n)(\n    input  logic                  clk,\n    input  logic                  rst,\n    input  logic                  ld,\n    input  logic                  en,\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]       w_top,\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]       a_left,\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_bot\n);\n\n    logic [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n    logic [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n    logic [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n    genvar i, j;\n    generate\n        for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_west\n            assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n        end\n        for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_edge\n            assign psum_v[0][j] = '0;\n            assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n            assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];\n        end\n        for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_pe_row\n            for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_pe_col\n                tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe (\n                    .clk        (clk),\n                    .rst        (rst),\n                    .ld         (ld),\n                    .en         (en),\n                    .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n                    .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n                    .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n                    .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n                    .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n                    .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j])\n                );\n            end\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu #(\n    parameter int N = 8,\n    parameter int DW = 8,\n    parameter int ACCW = 32\n)(\n    input  logic                  clk,\n    input  logic                  rst,\n    input  logic                  w_valid,\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]       w_data,\n    input  logic                  a_valid,\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]       a_data,\n    output logic                  c_valid,\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_data\n);\n\n    localparam int LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n    logic ld = w_valid;\n    logic en = ~w_valid;\n\n    logic [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n    genvar i;\n    generate\n        for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_skew\n            logic [DW-1:0] din;\n            assign din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : '0;\n            \n            if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n                assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n            end else begin : g_skewn\n                logic [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n                always_ff @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n                    if (rst) begin\n                        for (int k = 0; k < i; k++)\n                            sr[k] <= '0;\n                    end else if (en) begin\n                        sr[0] <= din;\n                        for (int k = 1; k < i; k++)\n                            sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n                    end\n                end\n                assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n            end\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;\n    tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array (\n        .clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n        .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot)\n    );\n\n    genvar j;\n    generate\n        for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_deskew\n            localparam int D = N - 1 - j;\n            logic [ACCW-1:0] cin;\n            assign cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];\n            \n            if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n                assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = cin;\n            end else begin : g_deskewn\n                logic [ACCW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n                always_ff @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n                    if (rst) begin\n                        for (int k = 0; k < D; k++)\n                            dr[k] <= '0;\n                    end else if (en) begin\n                        dr[0] <= cin;\n                        for (int k = 1; k < D; k++)\n                            dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n                    end\n                end\n                assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = dr[D-1];\n            end\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n    always_ff @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n        if (rst)\n            vpipe <= '0;\n        else if (en)\n            vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n    end\n    assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

Let me try a more explicit carry-lookahead adder approach.


  -> create_file(filename='design_opt4.sv', content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (Google TPU-style MXU)\n// Optimized: explicit 4-bit carry-lookahead adder blocks for 32-bit addition\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule cla4 (\n    input  logic [3:0] a,\n    input  logic [3:0] b,\n    input  logic       cin,\n    output logic [3:0] sum,\n    output logic       cout\n);\n    logic [3:0] g, p;\n    logic [3:0] c;\n    \n    assign g = a & b;\n    assign p = a ^ b;\n    \n    assign c[0] = cin;\n    assign c[1] = g[0] | (p[0] & cin);\n    assign c[2] = g[1] | (p[1] & g[0]) | (p[1] & p[0] & cin);\n    assign c[3] = g[2] | (p[2] & g[1]) | (p[2] & p[1] & g[0]) | (p[2] & p[1] & p[0] & cin);\n    assign cout = g[3] | (p[3] & g[2]) | (p[3] & p[2] & g[1]) | (p[3] & p[2] & p[1] & g[0]) | (p[3] & p[2] & p[1] & p[0] & cin);\n    \n    assign sum = p ^ c;\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule cla32 (\n    input  logic [31:0] a,\n    input  logic [31:0] b,\n    output logic [31:0] sum\n);\n    logic [7:0] c;\n    logic [3:0] sum_block [0:7];\n    \n    genvar k;\n    generate\n        for (k = 0; k < 8; k++) begin : cla_blocks\n            logic cin;\n            assign cin = (k == 0) ? 1'b0 : c[k-1];\n            \n            cla4 u_cla4 (\n                .a    (a[k*4 +: 4]),\n                .b    (b[k*4 +: 4]),\n                .cin  (cin),\n                .sum  (sum_block[k]),\n                .cout (c[k])\n            );\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    \n    assign sum = {sum_block[7], sum_block[6], sum_block[5], sum_block[4],\n                  sum_block[3], sum_block[2], sum_block[1], sum_block[0]};\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu_pe #(\n    parameter int DW = 8,\n    parameter int ACCW = 32\n)(\n    input  logic                  clk,\n    input  logic                  rst,\n    input  logic                  ld,\n    input logic [ACCW-1:0] sum;\n    cla32 u_cla32 (\n        .a   (psum_north),\n        .b   (prod_sext),\n        .sum (sum)\n    );\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n        if (rst) begin\n            w          <= '0;\n            a_east     <= '0;\n            psum_south <= '0;\n        end else begin\n            if (ld)\n                w <= w_north;\n            if (en) begin\n                a_east     <= a_west;\n                psum_south <= sum;\n            end\n        end\n    end\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu_array #(\n    parameter int N = 8,\n    parameter int DW = 8,\n    parameter int ACCW = 32\n)(\n    input  logic                  clk,\n    input  logic                  rst,\n    input  logic                  ld,\n    input  logic                  en,\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]       w_top,\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]       a_left,\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_bot\n);\n\n    logic [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n    logic [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n    logic [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n    genvar i, j;\n    generate\n        for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_west\n            assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n        end\n        for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_edge\n            assign psum_v[0][j] = '0;\n            assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n            assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];\n        end\n        for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_pe_row\n            for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_pe_col\n                tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe (\n          .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n                    .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n                    .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n                    .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n                    .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j])\n                );\n            end\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu #(\n    parameter int N = 8,\n    parameter int DW = 8,\n    parameter int TPU-style MXU)\n// Optimized: 2-stage pipelined adder in PE to reduce critical path\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe #(\n    parameter int DW = 8,\n    parameter int ACCW = 32\n)(\n    input  logic                  clk,\n    input  logic                  rst,\n    input  logic                  ld,\n    input  logic                  en,\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]         w_north,\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]         a_west,\n    input  logic [ACCW-1:0]       psum_north,\n    output logic [DW-1:0]         w_south,\n    output logic $signed(w);\n\n    // Sign-extended product\n    logic [ACCW-1:0] prod_sext;\n    assign prod_sext = {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n    // Split adder: lower 16 bits + upper 16 bits with assign sum_hi = psum_north[ACCW-1:2*DW] + sign_ext + carry_lo;\n    \n    logic [ACCW-1:0] sum;\n    assign sum = {sum_hi, sum_lo};\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n        if parameter int ACCW = 32\n)(\n    input  logic                  clk,\n    input  logic                  rst,\n    input  logic                  ld,\n    input  logic                  en,\n    input                  c_valid,\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_data\n);\n\n    localparam int LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n    logic ld = w_valid;\n    logic en = ~w_valid;\n\n    // Input skew: use simple shift TPU-style MXU)\n// Optimized: 16+16 split adder with explicit carry\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe #(\n    parameter int DW = 8,\n    parameter int ACCW = 32\n)(\n    input  logic                  clk,\n    input  logic                  rst,\n    input  logic                  ld,\n    input  logic                  en,\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]         w_north,\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]         a_west,\n    input  logic [ACCW-1:0]       psum_north,\n    output logic [DW-1:0]         w_south,\n    output logic [DW-1:0]         a_east,\n    output logic [ACCW-1:0]       psum_south\n);\n\n    logic [DW-1:0] w;\n    assign w_south = w;\n\n    logic signed [2*DW-1:0] prod;\n    assign prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n    // Split adder: lower 16 bits + upper 16 bits with carry\n    logic [2*DW-1:0] sum_lo;\n    logic            carry_lo;\n    logic [ACCW-2*DW-1:0] sum_hi;\n    \n    // Lower part: 16-bit adder\n    assign {carry_lo, sum_lo} = psum_north[2*DW-1:0] + prod;\n    \n    // Upper part: 16-bit adder with sign extension\n    logic [ACCW-2*DW-1:0] sign_ext;\n    assign sign_ext = {ACCW-2*DW{prod[2*DW-1]}};\n    assign sum_hi = psum_north[ACCW-1:2*DW] + sign_ext + carry_lo;\n    \n    logic [ACCW-1:0] sum;\n    assign sum = {sum_hi, sum_lo};\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n        if (rst) begin\n            w          <= '0;\n            a_east     <= '0;\n            psum_south <= '0;\n        end else begin\n            if (ld)\n                w <= w_north;\n            if (en) begin\n                a_east     <= a_west;\n                psum_south <= sum;\n            end\n        end\n    end\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu_array #(\n    parameter int N = 8,\n    parameter int DW = 8,\n    parameter int ACCW = 32\n)(\n    input  logic                  clk,\n    input  logic                  rst,\n    input  logic                  ld,\n    input  logic                  en,\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]       w_top,\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]       a_left,\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_bot\n);\n\n    logic [DW-1:0]   a_h      assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];\n        end\n        for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_pe_row\n            for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_pe_col\n                tpu_pe .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n                    .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j])\n                );\n            end\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu #(\n    parameter int N = 8,\n    parameter int DW = 8,\n    parameter int ACCW = 32\n)(\n    input  logic                  clk,\n    input  logic                  rst,\n    input  logic                  w_valid,\n    input c_data\n);\n\n    localparam int LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n    logic ld = w_valid;\n    logic en = ~w_valid;\n\n    // Input skew\n    logic [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n    logic [DW-1:0] skew_sr [0:N-1][0:N-1];\n    \n    genvar i;\n    generate\n        for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_skew\n            logic [DW-1:0] din;\n            assign din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : '0;\n            \n            if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n                assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n            end else begin : g_skewn\n                always_ff @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n                    if (rst) begin\n                        for (int k = 0; k < i; k++)\n                            skew_sr[i][k] <= '0;\n                    end else if (en) begin\n                        skew_sr[i][0] generate\n        for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_deskew\n            localparam int D = N - 1 - j;\n            logic [ACCW-1:0] cin;\n            assign cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];\n begin\n        if (rst)\n            vpipe <= '0;\n        else if (en)\n            vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n    end\n    assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

int DW = 8,\n    parameter int ACCW = 32\n)(\n    input  logic                  clk,\n    input  logic                  rst,\n    input  logic                  ld,\n    input  logic                  en,\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]         w_north,\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]         a_west,\n    input  logic [ACCW-1:0]       psum_north,\n    output logic [DW-1:0]         w_south,\n    output logic '0;\n        end else begin\n            if (ld)\n                w <= w_north;\n            if (en) begin\n                a_east     <= a_west;\n                psum_south <= sum;\n            end\n        end\n    end\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu_array #(\n    parameter int N = 8,\n    parameter int DW = 8,\n    parameter int ACCW = 32\n)(\n    input  logic                  clk,\n    input  logic                  rst,\n    input  logic                  ld,\n    input  logic                  en,\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]       w_top,\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]       a_left,\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_bot\n);\n\n    logic [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n    logic [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n    logic [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n    genvar i, j;\n       for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_pe_col\n                tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe (\n                    .clk        (clk),\n                    .rst        (rst),\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu #(\n    parameter int N = 8,\n    parameter int DW = 8,\n    parameter int ACCW = 32\n)(\n    input  logic                  clk,\n    input  logic                  rst,\n    input  logic                  w_valid,\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]       w_data,\n    input  logic                  a_valid,\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]       a_data,\n    output logic                  c_valid,\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_data\n);\n\n    localparam int LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n    logic ld = w_valid;\n    logic en = ~w_valid;\n\n    // Input skew: use single shift register per row but with shared storage\n    logic [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n    logic [DW-1:0] skew_sr [0:N-1][0:N-2];  // Max delay is N-1\n    \n    genvar i;\n    generate\n        for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_skew\n            logic [DW-1:0] din;\n            assign din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : '0;\n            \n            if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n                assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n            end else begin : g_skewn\n                always_ff @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n                    if (rst) begin\n                        for (int k = 0; k < i; k++)\n                            skew_sr[i][k] <= '0;\n                    end else if (en) begin\n                        skew_sr[i][0] <= din;\n                        for (int k = 1; k < i; k++)\n                            skew_sr[i][k] <= skew_sr[i][k-1];\n                    end\n                end\n                assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = skew_sr[i][i-1];\n            end\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;\n    tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array (\n        .clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n        .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot)\n    );\n\n    // Output deskew: use single array with max delay\n    logic [ACCW-1:0] deskew_sr [0:N-1][0:N-2];\n    \n    genvar j;\n    generate\n        for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_deskew\n            localparam int D = N - 1 - j;\n            logic [ACCW-1:0] cin;\n            assign cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];\n begin\n                    if (rst) begin\n                        for (int k = 0; k < D; k++)\n                            deskew_sr[j][k] <= '0;\n                    end else if (en) begin\n                        deskew_sr[j][0] <= cin;\n                        for (int k = 1; k < D; k++)\n                            deskew_sr[j][k] <= deskew_sr[j][k-1];\n                    end\n                end\n                assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = deskew_sr[j][D-1];\n            end\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n    always_ff @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n        if (rst)\n            vpipe <= '0;\n        else if (en)\n            vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n    end\n    assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

[ASSISTANT]
  -> create_file(filename='design_opt17.sv', content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (Google TPU-style MXU)\n// Optimized: use simple ripple adder structure, optimize PE for synthesis\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe #(\n    parameter int DW = 8,\n    parameter int ACCW = 32\n)(\n    input  logic                  clk,\n    input  logic                  rst,\n    input  logic                  ld,\n    input  logic                  en,\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]         w_north,\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]         a_west,\n    input  logic [ACCW-1:0]       psum_north,\n    output logic [DW-1:0]         w_south,\n    output logic [DW-1:0]         a_east,\n    output logic [ACCW-1:0]       psum_south\n);\n\n    logic [DW-1:0] w;\n    assign w_south = w;\n\n    logic signed [2*DW-1:0] prod;\n    assign prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n    // Sign-extended product\n    logic [ACCW-1:0] prod_sext;\n    assign prod_sext = {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n    // Ripple-carry style adder (explicit structure)\n    logic [ACCW-1:0] sum;\n    logic [ACCW:0] carry;\n    assign carry[0] = 1'b0;\n    \n    genvar k;\n    generate\n        for (k = 0; k < ACCW; k++) begin : ripple\n            assign sum[k] = psum_north[k] ^ prod_sext[k] ^ carry[k];\n            assign carry[k+1] = (psum_north[k] & prod_sext[k]) | \n                                (psum_north[k] & carry[k]) | \n                                (prod_sext[k] & carry[k]);\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n        if (rst) begin\n            w          <= '0;\n            a_east     <= '0;\n            psum_south <= '0;\n        end else begin\n            if (ld)\n                w <= w_north;\n            if (en) begin\n                a_east     <= a_west;\n                psum_south <= sum;\n            end\n        end\n    end\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu_array #(\n    parameter int N = 8,\n    parameter int DW = 8,\n    parameter int ACCW = 32\n)(\n    input  logic                  clk,\n    input  logic                  rst,\n    input  logic                  ld,\n    input  logic                  en,\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]       w_top,\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]       a_left,\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_bot\n);\n\n    logic [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n    logic [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n    logic [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n    genvar i, j;\n    generate\n        for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_west\n            assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n        end\n        for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_edge\n            assign psum_v[0][j] = '0;\n            assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n            assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];\n        end\n        for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_pe_row\n            for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_pe_col\n                tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe (\n                    .clk        (clk),\n                    .rst        (rst),\n                    .ld         (ld),\n                    .en         (en),\n                    .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n                    .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n                    .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n                    .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n                    .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n                    .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j])\n                );\n            end\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu #(\n    parameter int N = 8,\n    parameter int DW = 8,\n    parameter int ACCW = 32\n)(\n    input  logic                  clk,\n    input  logic                  rst,\n    input  logic                  w_valid,\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]       w_data,\n    input  logic                  a_valid,\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]       a_data,\n    output logic                  c_valid,\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_data\n);\n\n    localparam int LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n    logic ld = w_valid;\n    logic en = ~w_valid;\n\n    // Input skew\n    logic [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n    logic [DW-1:0] skew_sr [0:N-1][0:N-2];\n    \n    genvar i;\n    generate\n        for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_skew\n            logic [DW-1:0] din;\n            assign din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : '0;\n            \n            if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n                assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n            end else begin : g_skewn\n                always_ff @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n                    if (rst) begin\n                        for (int k = 0; k < i; k++)\n                            skew_sr[i][k] <= '0;\n                    end else if (en) begin\n                        skew_sr[i][0] <= din;\n                        for (int k = 1; k < i; k++)\n                            skew_sr[i][k] <= skew_sr[i][k-1];\n                    end\n                end\n                assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = skew_sr[i][i-1];\n            end\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;\n    tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array (\n        .clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n        .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot)\n    );\n\n    // Output deskew\n    logic [ACCW-1:0] deskew_sr [0:N-1][0:N-2];\n    \n    genvar j;\n    generate\n        for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_deskew\n            localparam int D = N - 1 - j;\n            logic [ACCW-1:0] cin;\n            assign cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];\n            \n            if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n                assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = cin;\n            end else begin : g_deskewn\n                always_ff @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n                    if (rst) begin\n                        for (int k = 0; k < D; k++)\n                            deskew_sr[j][k] <= '0;\n                    end else if (en) begin\n                        deskew_sr[j][0] <= cin;\n                        for (int k = 1; k < D; k++)\n                            deskew_sr[j][k] <= deskew_sr[j][k-1];\n                    end\n                end\n                assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = deskew_sr[j][D-1];\n            end\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n    always_ff @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n        if (rst)\n            vpipe <= '0;\n        else if (en)\n            vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n    end\n    assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

logic                  rst,\n    input  logic                  w_valid,\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]       w_data,\n    input  logic                  a_valid,\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]       a_data,\n    output logic                  c_valid,\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_data\n);\n\n    localparam int LAT = 2*N - 1;\n    localparam int SKEW_DEPTH = N*(N-1)/2;  // 28 for N=8\n    localparam int DESKEW_DEPTH = N*(N-1)/2; // 28 for N=8\n\n    logic ld = w_valid;\n    logic en = ~w_valid;\n\n    // Input skew: single flat shift register\n    logic [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n    logic [DW-1:0] skew_sr [0:SKEW_DEPTH-1];\n    logic [DW-1:0] skew_in [0:N-1];\n    logic [DW-1:0] skew_out [0:N-1];\n    \n    // Input mapping\n    genvar i;\n    generate\n        for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_skew_in\n            assign skew_in[i] = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : '0;\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    \n    // Skew shift register - single flat array\n    always_ff @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n        if (rst) begin\n            for (int k = 0; k < SKEW_DEPTH; k++)\n                skew_sr[k] <= '0;\n        end else if (en) begin\n            // Row 0: no delay\n            // Row 1: 1 cycle delay (skew_sr[0])\n            // Row 2: 2 cycle delay (skew_sr[1], skew_sr[2])\n            // ...\n            int idx = 0;\n            for (int row = 1; row < N; row++) begin\n                skew_sr[idx] <= skew_in[row];\n                for (int k = 1; k < row; k++) begin\n                    skew_sr[idx + k] <= skew_sr[idx + k - 1];\n                end\n                idx += row;\n            end\n        end\n    end\n    \n    // Output mapping\n    generate\n        for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_skew_out\n            if (i == 0) begin\n                assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = skew_in[0];\n            end else begin\n                localparam int BASE = i*(i-1)/2;\n                assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = skew_sr[BASE + i - 1];\n            end\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;\n    tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array (\n        .clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n        .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot)\n    );\n\n    // Output deskew: single flat shift register\n    logic [ACCW-1:0] deskew_sr [0:DESKEW_DEPTH-1];\n    logic [ACCW-1:0] deskew_in [0:N-1];\n    logic [ACCW-1:0] deskew_out [0:N-1];\n    \n    generate\n        for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_deskew_in\n            assign deskew_in[j] = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    \n    always_ff @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n        if (rst) begin\n            for (int k = 0; k < DESKEW_DEPTH; k++)\n                deskew_sr[k] <= '0;\n        end else if (en) begin\n            int idx = 0;\n            for (int col = 0; col < N-1; col++) begin\n                int D = N - 1 - col;\n                deskew_sr[idx] <= deskew_in[col];\n                for (int k = 1; k < D; k++) begin\n                    deskew_sr[idx + k] <= deskew_sr[idx + k - 1];\n                end\n                idx += D;\n            end\n        end\n    end\n    \n    generate\n        for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_deskew_out\n            localparam int D = N - 1 - j;\n            if (D == 0) begin\n                assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = deskew_in[j];\n            end else begin\n                localparam int BASE = (N-1-j)*(N-j)/2 - D;\n                assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = deskew_sr[BASE + D - 1];\n            end\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n    always_ff @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n        if (rst)\n            vpipe <= '0;\n        else if (en)\n            vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n    end\n    assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

= 8,\n    parameter int ACCW = 32\n)(\n    input  logic                  clk,\n    input  logic                  rst,\n    input  logic                  ld,\n    input  logic                  en,\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]         w_north,\n    input  logic [DW-1:0]         a_west,\n    input  logic [ACCW-1:0]       psum_north,\n    output logic [DW-1:0]         w_south,\n    output logic [DW-1:0]         a_east,\n    output logic [ACCW-1:0]       psum_south\n);\n\n    logic [DW-1:0] w;\n    assign w_south = w;\n\n    logic signed [2*DW-1:0] prod;\n    assign prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n    // Sign-extended product\n    logic [ACCW-1:0] prod_sext;\n    assign prod_sext = {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n    // Simple adder\n    logic [ACCW-1:0] sum;\n    assign sum = psum_north + prod_sext;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n        if (rst) begin\n            w          <= '0;\n            a_east     <= '0;\n            psum_south <= '0;\n        end else begin\n            if (ld)\n                w <= w_north;\n            if (en) begin\n                a_east     <= a_west;\n                psum_south <= sum;\n            end\n        end\n    end\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu_array #(\n    parameter int N = 8,\n    parameter int DW = 8,\n    parameter int ACCW = 32\n)(\n    input  logic                  clk,\n    input  logic                  rst,\n    input  logic                  ld,\n    input  logic                  en,\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]       w_top,\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]       a_left,\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_bot\n);\n\n    logic [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n    logic [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n    logic [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n    genvar i, j;\n    generate\n        for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_west\n            assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n        end\n        for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_edge\n            assign psum_v[0][j] = '0;\n            assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n            assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];\n        end\n        for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_pe_row\n            for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_pe_col\n                tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe (\n                    .clk        (clk),\n                    .rst        (rst),\n                    .ld         (ld),\n                    .en         (en),\n                    .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n                    .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n                    .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n                    .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n                    .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n                    .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j])\n                );\n            end\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu #(\n    parameter int N = 8,\n    parameter int DW = 8,\n    parameter int ACCW = 32\n)(\n    input  logic                  clk,\n    input  logic                  rst,\n    input  logic                  w_valid,\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]       w_data,\n    input  logic                  a_valid,\n    input  logic [N*DW-1:0]       a_data,\n    output logic                  c_valid,\n    output logic [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_data\n);\n\n    localparam int LAT = 2*N - 1;\n    localparam int SKEW_DEPTH = N*(N-1)/2;  // 28 for N=8\n    localparam int DESKEW_DEPTH = N*(N-1)/2; // 28 for N=8\n\n    logic ld = w_valid;\n    logic en = ~w_valid;\n\n    // Input skew: single flat shift register\n    logic [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n    logic [DW-1:0] skew_sr [0:SKEW_DEPTH-1];\n    logic [DW-1:0] skew_in [0:N-1];\n    \n    // Input mapping\n    genvar i;\n    generate\n        for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_skew_in\n            assign skew_in[i] = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : '0;\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    \n    // Skew shift register - single flat array\n    always_ff @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n        if (rst) begin\n            for (int k = 0; k < SKEW_DEPTH; k++)\n                skew_sr[k] <= '0;\n        end else if (en) begin\n            int idx = 0;\n            for (int row = 1; row < N; row++) begin\n                skew_sr[idx] <= skew_in[row];\n                for (int k = 1; k < row; k++) begin\n                    skew_sr[idx + k] <= skew_sr[idx + k - 1];\n                end\n                idx += row;\n            end\n        end\n    end\n    \n    // Output mapping\n    generate\n        for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_skew_out_map\n            if (i == 0) begin : g_skew_out_0\n                assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = skew_in[0];\n            end else begin : g_skew_out_n\n                localparam int BASE = i*(i-1)/2;\n                assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = skew_sr[BASE + i - 1];\n            end\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;\n    tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array (\n        .clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n        .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot)\n    );\n\n    // Output deskew: single flat shift register\n    logic [ACCW-1:0] deskew_sr [0:DESKEW_DEPTH-1];\n    logic [ACCW-1:0] deskew_in [0:N-1];\n    \n    generate\n        for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_deskew_in\n            assign deskew_in[j] = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    \n    always_ff @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n        if (rst) begin\n            for (int k = 0; k < DESKEW_DEPTH; k++)\n                deskew_sr[k] <= '0;\n        end else if (en) begin\n            int idx = 0;\n            for (int col = 0; col < N-1; col++) begin\n                int D = N - 1 - col;\n                deskew_sr[idx] <= deskew_in[col];\n                for (int k = 1; k < D; k++) begin\n                    deskew_sr[idx + k] <= deskew_sr[idx + k - 1];\n                end\n                idx += D;\n            end\n        end\n    end\n    \n    // Output mapping for deskew - use if-else instead of localparam in generate\n    generate\n        for (j = 0; j < N; j++) begin : g_deskew_out_map\n            if (j == N-1) begin : g_deskew_out_last\n                assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = deskew_in[j];\n            end else begin : g_deskew_out_other\n                localparam int D = N - 1 - j;\n                localparam int BASE = (N-1-j)*(N-j)/2 - D;\n                assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = deskew_sr[BASE + D - 1];\n            end\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n    always_ff @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n        if (rst)\n            vpipe <= '0;\n        else if (en)\n            vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n    end\n    assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

[ASSISTANT]
## Summary

### 1. Approaches Tried & Best Result
- **Best design**: `design_opt11.sv` (same as `design_opt6.sv`) with **4.78M area_delay_product** (1028ps delay × 4650 area)
- Evaluated 20+ variants including explicit adders (CLA, Brent-Kung, carry-skip, ripple), pipelined multipliers, split adders, and skew/deskew optimizations

### 2. Most Impactful Optimizations
- **Flattened shift registers** for input skew/output deskew (single 28-deep array vs per-row/col arrays) - reduced area
- **Separate always blocks** for weight register vs compute path in PE - reduced mux complexity on critical path
- **Simple `+` operator** for 32-bit accumulation - synthesis optimizes better than explicit adder structures
- **Target delay 200-300ps** - best area/delay tradeoff for this library

### 3. Regressions / Failures
| Approach | Result |
|----------|--------|
| Explicit CLA/Brent-Kung/carry-skip adders | +15-20% area, no delay improvement |
| Explicit ripple-carry generate | +4% area, +3% delay |
| 2-stage pipelined multiplier | **Broke cycle accuracy** (latency mismatch) |
| Conditional assignment `(en) ? a+b : a` | Slightly worse |
| 16+16 split adder | Worse delay (1087ps) |

### 4. Lessons Learned
- **Trust synthesis** for adder optimization - explicit structures hurt more than help in ASAP7
- **Critical path** is the 32-bit accumulation chain through multiplier→sign-extend→adder→register
- **Skew/deskew** is off the critical path; flattening saves area without timing cost
- **Cycle accuracy is fragile** - any pipeline depth change breaks the 15-cycle latency contract
- **Target delay 200-300ps** consistently gave best ADP; higher targets increased delay without proportional area savings

The final best file is `design_opt11.sv` (or `design_opt6.sv`).
